===== apps/web/src/app/providers.tsx =====

// web/src/app/providers.tsx

import type { JSX, PropsWithChildren } from "react";
import { AuthProvider } from "../core/auth/authContext";
import { TenantThemeProvider } from "../core/tenant/tenantThemeProvider";
import { TenantProvider } from "../core/tenant/tenantContext";

export function AppProviders({ children }: PropsWithChildren): JSX.Element {
  return (
    <AuthProvider>
      <TenantProvider>
        <TenantThemeProvider>{children}</TenantThemeProvider>
      </TenantProvider>
    </AuthProvider>
  );
}

// apps/web/src/app/router.tsx

import { createBrowserRouter, Navigate } from "react-router-dom";

import ProtectedRoute from "../core/routing/protectedRoute";
import SuperAdminRoute from "../core/routing/superAdminRoute";
import TenantRoute from "../core/routing/tenantRoute";

import AuthLayout from "../layouts/authLayout";
import SuperAdminLayout from "../layouts/superAdminLayout";
import TenantLayout from "../layouts/tenantLayout";

import LoginPage from "../features/auth/pages/LoginPage";
import SuperAdminDashboard from "../features/superadmin/pages/dashboard";
import TenantAdminDashboard from "../features/tenant-admin/pages/dashboard";

import AccessDenied from "../pages/accessDenied";
import NotFound from "../pages/notFound";
import { EditarUsuarioPage, MinhaSenhaPage, NovoUsuarioPage, UsuarioDetailsPage, UsuariosListPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/usuarios";
import { ConfiguracoesLocatarioPage, MeuLocatarioPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/locatario";
import { AssinaturaModulosPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/assinatura";
import { ClienteDetailsPage, ClientesListPage, EditarClientePage, NovoClientePage } from "../features/tenant-admin/clientes";
import { EditarFornecedorPage, FornecedorDetailsPage, FornecedoresListPage, NovoFornecedorPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/fornecedores";
import { EditarProdutoPage, NovoProdutoPage, ProdutoDetailsPage, ProdutosListPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/produtos";
import { NovaVendaPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/vendas";
import { TransacaoDetailsPage, TransacoesListPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/transacoes";
import { NovaCompraPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/compras";
import { EditarLocatarioPage, LocatarioDetailsPage, LocatariosListPage, NovoLocatarioPage, NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage } from "../features/superadmin/locatarios";
import PlataformaLoginPage from "../features/auth/pages/PlataformaLoginPage";
import UsuariosLocatarioListPage from "../features/superadmin/locatarios/pages/UsuariosLocatarioListPage";
import { FinanceiroPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/financeiro";
import { NovaOrdemServicoPage, OrdemServicoDetailsPage, OrdensServicoListPage } from "../features/tenant-admin/ordens-servico";

/**
 * 
 */
export const router = createBrowserRouter([
  {
    path: "/",
    element: <Navigate to="/login" replace />,
  },
  {
    element: <AuthLayout />,
    children: [
      {
        path: "/login",
        element: <LoginPage />,
      },
      {
        path: "/plataforma/login",
        element: <PlataformaLoginPage />,
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    element: <ProtectedRoute />,
    children: [
      {
        path: "/acesso-negado",
        element: <AccessDenied />,
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    element: <SuperAdminRoute loginPath="/plataforma/login" />,
    children: [
      {
        path: "/plataforma",
        element: <SuperAdminLayout />,
        children: [
          { 
            index: true, 
            element: <SuperAdminDashboard /> 
          },
          { 
            path: "locatarios", 
            element: <LocatariosListPage /> 
          },
          { 
            path: "locatarios/novo", 
            element: <NovoLocatarioPage /> 
          },
          { 
            path: "locatarios/:locatarioId", 
            element: <LocatarioDetailsPage /> 
          },
          { 
            path: "locatarios/:locatarioId/editar", 
            element: <EditarLocatarioPage /> 
          },
          { 
            path: "locatarios/:locatarioId/usuarios", 
            element: <UsuariosLocatarioListPage /> 
          },
          { 
            path: "locatarios/:locatarioId/usuarios/novo", 
            element: <NovoUsuarioLocatarioPage /> 
          },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    element: <TenantRoute />,
    children: [
      {
        path: "/app",
        element: <TenantLayout />,
        children: [
          {
            index: true,
            element: <TenantAdminDashboard />,
          },
          {
            path: "usuarios",
            element: <UsuariosListPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "usuarios/novo",
            element: <NovoUsuarioPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "usuarios/:usuarioId",
            element: <UsuarioDetailsPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "usuarios/:usuarioId/editar",
            element: <EditarUsuarioPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "locatario",
            element: <MeuLocatarioPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "locatario/configuracoes",
            element: <ConfiguracoesLocatarioPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "minha-senha",
            element: <MinhaSenhaPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "assinatura/modulos",
            element: <AssinaturaModulosPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "clientes",
            element: <ClientesListPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "clientes/novo",
            element: <NovoClientePage />,
          },
          {
            path: "clientes/:clienteId",
            element: <ClienteDetailsPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "clientes/:clienteId/editar",
            element: <EditarClientePage />,
          },
          {
            path: "fornecedores",
            element: <FornecedoresListPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "fornecedores/novo",
            element: <NovoFornecedorPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "fornecedores/:fornecedorId",
            element: <FornecedorDetailsPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "fornecedores/:fornecedorId/editar",
            element: <EditarFornecedorPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "transacoes",
            element: <TransacoesListPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "transacoes/:transacaoId",
            element: <TransacaoDetailsPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "produtos",
            element: <ProdutosListPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "produtos/novo",
            element: <NovoProdutoPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "produtos/:produtoId",
            element: <ProdutoDetailsPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "produtos/:produtoId/editar",
            element: <EditarProdutoPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "vendas/nova",
            element: <NovaVendaPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "compras/nova",
            element: <NovaCompraPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "financeiro",
            element: <FinanceiroPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "ordens-servico",
            element: <OrdensServicoListPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "ordens-servico/nova",
            element: <NovaOrdemServicoPage />,
          },
          {
            path: "ordens-servico/:ordemServicoId",
            element: <OrdemServicoDetailsPage />,
          },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    path: "*",
    element: <NotFound />,
  },
]);

===== apps/web/src/core/api/endpoints.ts =====



===== apps/web/src/core/api/httpClient.ts =====

// web/src/core/api/httpClient.ts

export type HttpMethod = "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE";

export type HttpRequestConfig = {
  method?: HttpMethod;
  headers?: Record<string, string>;
  body?: unknown;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  credentials?: RequestCredentials;
  skipAuthRetry?: boolean;
};

export type ApiErrorCode =
  | "NAO_AUTORIZADO"
  | "NAO_AUTENTICADO"
  | "INVALIDO"
  | "NAO_ENCONTRADO"
  | "ERRO_INTERNO"
  | "CONFLITO";

export type ApiErrorEnvelope = {
  erro?: {
    codigo?: string;
    mensagem?: string;
    detalhes?: Record<string, unknown>;
  };
};

export class HttpError extends Error {
  public readonly status: number;
  public readonly code?: string;
  public readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;

  constructor(params: {
    message: string;
    status: number;
    code?: string;
    details?: Record<string, unknown>;
  }) {
    super(params.message);
    this.name = "HttpError";
    this.status = params.status;
    this.code = params.code;
    this.details = params.details;
  }
}

const API_BASE_URL = (
  import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL ?? "https://corekit-api-homolog.onrender.com/api"
).replace(/\/+$/, "");

let accessTokenGetter: (() => string | null) | null = null;
let refreshSessionHandler: (() => Promise<boolean>) | null = null;
let unauthorizedHandler: (() => void) | null = null;

export function registerAccessTokenGetter(getter: () => string | null): void {
  accessTokenGetter = getter;
}

export function registerRefreshSessionHandler(handler: (() => Promise<boolean>) | null): void {
  refreshSessionHandler = handler;
}

export function registerUnauthorizedHandler(handler: (() => void) | null): void {
  unauthorizedHandler = handler;
}

function buildUrl(path: string): string {
  if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(path)) {
    return path;
  }

  return `${API_BASE_URL}${path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`}`;
}

function isJsonResponse(response: Response): boolean {
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  return contentType.includes("application/json");
}

async function buildHttpError(response: Response): Promise<HttpError> {
  if (isJsonResponse(response)) {
    const data = (await response.json()) as ApiErrorEnvelope;

    return new HttpError({
      message: data.erro?.mensagem?.trim() || `Erro HTTP ${response.status}`,
      status: response.status,
      code: data.erro?.codigo,
      details: data.erro?.detalhes,
    });
  }

  const text = await response.text().catch(() => "");
  return new HttpError({
    message: text || `Erro HTTP ${response.status}`,
    status: response.status,
  });
}

function isFormDataBody(body: unknown): body is FormData {
  return typeof FormData !== "undefined" && body instanceof FormData;
}

function buildHeaders(config: HttpRequestConfig, token: string | null): Record<string, string> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {
    Accept: "application/json",
    ...config.headers,
  };

  const hasBody = config.body !== undefined && config.body !== null;

  if (hasBody && !isFormDataBody(config.body)) {
    headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";
  }

  // Permite desabilitar a injeção automática com `Authorization: ""`,
  // útil em refresh/logout.
  if (token && headers.Authorization !== "") {
    headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  }

  return headers;
}

function buildBody(body: unknown): BodyInit | undefined {
  if (body === undefined || body === null) {
    return undefined;
  }

  if (isFormDataBody(body)) {
    return body;
  }

  return JSON.stringify(body);
}

async function parseSuccessResponse<TResponse>(response: Response): Promise<TResponse> {
  if (response.status === 204) {
    return undefined as TResponse;
  }

  if (isJsonResponse(response)) {
    return (await response.json()) as TResponse;
  }

  return (await response.text()) as TResponse;
}

let refreshPromise: Promise<boolean> | null = null;

async function ensureRefreshedSession(): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!refreshSessionHandler) {
    return false;
  }

  if (!refreshPromise) {
    refreshPromise = (async () => {
      try {
        return await refreshSessionHandler();
      } finally {
        refreshPromise = null;
      }
    })();
  }

  return refreshPromise;
}

async function rawFetch(path: string, config: HttpRequestConfig): Promise<Response> {
  const token = accessTokenGetter?.() ?? null;

  return fetch(buildUrl(path), {
    method: config.method ?? "GET",
    headers: buildHeaders(config, token),
    body: buildBody(config.body),
    signal: config.signal,
    credentials: config.credentials ?? "include",
  });
}

export async function httpRequest<TResponse>(path: string, config: HttpRequestConfig = {}): Promise<TResponse> {
  let response = await rawFetch(path, config);

  if (response.status === 401 && !config.skipAuthRetry) {
    const refreshed = await ensureRefreshedSession();

    if (refreshed) {
      response = await rawFetch(path, {
        ...config,
        skipAuthRetry: true,
      });
    } else {
      unauthorizedHandler?.();
    }
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw await buildHttpError(response);
  }

  return parseSuccessResponse<TResponse>(response);
}

export const httpClient = {
  get<TResponse>(path: string, config?: Omit<HttpRequestConfig, "method" | "body">) {
    return httpRequest<TResponse>(path, {
      ...config,
      method: "GET",
    });
  },

  post<TResponse, TBody = unknown>(path: string, body?: TBody, config?: Omit<HttpRequestConfig, "method" | "body">) {
    return httpRequest<TResponse>(path, {
      ...config,
      method: "POST",
      body,
    });
  },

  put<TResponse, TBody = unknown>(path: string, body?: TBody, config?: Omit<HttpRequestConfig, "method" | "body">) {
    return httpRequest<TResponse>(path, {
      ...config,
      method: "PUT",
      body,
    });
  },

  patch<TResponse, TBody = unknown>(path: string, body?: TBody, config?: Omit<HttpRequestConfig, "method" | "body">) {
    return httpRequest<TResponse>(path, {
      ...config,
      method: "PATCH",
      body,
    });
  },

  delete<TResponse>(path: string, config?: Omit<HttpRequestConfig, "method" | "body">) {
    return httpRequest<TResponse>(path, {
      ...config,
      method: "DELETE",
    });
  },
};


===== apps/web/src/core/api/interceptors.ts =====



===== apps/web/src/core/api/permissions.ts =====

// web/src/core/api/permissions.ts

import type { StatusUsuario } from "../../features/tenant-admin/usuarios/tipos";

export const USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS = {
  LISTAR: "tenant.usuario.listar",
  CRIAR: "tenant.usuario.criar",
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.usuario.atualizar",
} as const;

export const USUARIOS_MODULE = "USUARIOS" as const;
export const CLIENTES_MODULE = "CLIENTES" as const;
export const FORNECEDORES_MODULE = "FORNECEDORES" as const;
export const PRODUTOS_MODULE = "PRODUTOS" as const;
export const TRANSACOES_MODULE = "TRANSACOES" as const;
export const ORDENS_SERVICO_MODULE = "ORDENS_SERVICO" as const;

export const CLIENTES_PERMISSIONS = {
  LISTAR: "tenant.cliente.listar",
  CRIAR: "tenant.cliente.criar",
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.cliente.atualizar",
  EXCLUIR: "tenant.cliente.excluir",
} as const;

export const FORNECEDORES_PERMISSIONS = {
  LISTAR: "tenant.fornecedor.listar",
  CRIAR: "tenant.fornecedor.criar",
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.fornecedor.atualizar",
  EXCLUIR: "tenant.fornecedor.excluir",
} as const;

export const PRODUTOS_PERMISSIONS = {
  LISTAR: "tenant.produto.listar",
  CRIAR: "tenant.produto.criar",
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.produto.atualizar",
  EXCLUIR: "tenant.produto.excluir",
} as const;

export const TRANSACOES_PERMISSIONS = {
  LISTAR: "tenant.transacao.listar",
  CRIAR: "tenant.transacao.criar",
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.transacao.atualizar",
  CANCELAR: "tenant.transacao.cancelar",
  CONCLUIR: "tenant.transacao.concluir",
} as const;

export const ORDENS_SERVICO_PERMISSIONS = {
  LISTAR: "tenant.ordens_servico.ler",
  CRIAR: "tenant.ordens_servico.criar",
  ATUALIZAR: "tenant.ordens_servico.atualizar",
  CANCELAR: "tenant.ordens_servico.cancelar",
  CONCLUIR: "tenant.ordens_servico.concluir",
  GERAR_VENDA: "tenant.ordens_servico.gerar_venda",
} as const;

export type UsuarioAction =
  | "listar"
  | "ver"
  | "criar"
  | "editar"
  | "inativar"
  | "reativar";

export type FrontendAuthorizationContext = {
  permissions?: string[];
  modules?: string[];
  isSuperAdmin?: boolean;
};

function normalizarLista(values?: string[]) {
  return (values ?? []).map((value) => value.trim().toUpperCase());
}

export function hasPermission(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
  permission: string,
): boolean {
  if (ctx.isSuperAdmin) return true;
  return normalizarLista(ctx.permissions).includes(permission.trim().toUpperCase());
}

export function hasModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
  moduleCode: string,
): boolean {
  if (ctx.isSuperAdmin) return true;
  return normalizarLista(ctx.modules).includes(moduleCode.trim().toUpperCase());
}

export function canAccessUsuariosModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return (
    hasModule(ctx, USUARIOS_MODULE) &&
    hasPermission(ctx, USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.LISTAR)
  );
}

function canReadCrudModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
  moduleCode: string,
  listPermission: string,
): boolean {
  return hasModule(ctx, moduleCode) && hasPermission(ctx, listPermission);
}

function canWriteCrudModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
  moduleCode: string,
  writePermission: string,
): boolean {
  return hasModule(ctx, moduleCode) && hasPermission(ctx, writePermission);
}

export function canAccessClientesModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canReadCrudModule(ctx, CLIENTES_MODULE, CLIENTES_PERMISSIONS.LISTAR);
}

export function canCreateCliente(ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(ctx, CLIENTES_MODULE, CLIENTES_PERMISSIONS.CRIAR);
}

export function canEditCliente(ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    CLIENTES_MODULE,
    CLIENTES_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR,
  );
}

export function canAccessFornecedoresModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canReadCrudModule(
    ctx,
    FORNECEDORES_MODULE,
    FORNECEDORES_PERMISSIONS.LISTAR,
  );
}

export function canCreateFornecedor(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    FORNECEDORES_MODULE,
    FORNECEDORES_PERMISSIONS.CRIAR,
  );
}

export function canEditFornecedor(ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    FORNECEDORES_MODULE,
    FORNECEDORES_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR,
  );
}

export function canAccessProdutosModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canReadCrudModule(ctx, PRODUTOS_MODULE, PRODUTOS_PERMISSIONS.LISTAR);
}

export function canCreateProduto(ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(ctx, PRODUTOS_MODULE, PRODUTOS_PERMISSIONS.CRIAR);
}

export function canEditProduto(ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    PRODUTOS_MODULE,
    PRODUTOS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR,
  );
}

export function canAccessTransacoesModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canReadCrudModule(
    ctx,
    TRANSACOES_MODULE,
    TRANSACOES_PERMISSIONS.LISTAR,
  );
}

export function canCreateTransacao(ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    TRANSACOES_MODULE,
    TRANSACOES_PERMISSIONS.CRIAR,
  );
}

export function canEditTransacao(ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    TRANSACOES_MODULE,
    TRANSACOES_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR,
  );
}

export function canCancelTransacao(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    TRANSACOES_MODULE,
    TRANSACOES_PERMISSIONS.CANCELAR,
  );
}

export function canConcludeTransacao(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    TRANSACOES_MODULE,
    TRANSACOES_PERMISSIONS.CONCLUIR,
  );
}

export function canAccessOrdensServicoModule(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canReadCrudModule(
    ctx,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_MODULE,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_PERMISSIONS.LISTAR,
  );
}

export function canCreateOrdemServico(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_MODULE,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_PERMISSIONS.CRIAR,
  );
}

export function canEditOrdemServico(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_MODULE,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR,
  );
}

export function canCancelOrdemServico(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_MODULE,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_PERMISSIONS.CANCELAR,
  );
}

export function canConcludeOrdemServico(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_MODULE,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_PERMISSIONS.CONCLUIR,
  );
}

export function canGenerateVendaFromOrdemServico(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return canWriteCrudModule(
    ctx,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_MODULE,
    ORDENS_SERVICO_PERMISSIONS.GERAR_VENDA,
  );
}

export function canCreateUsuario(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return (
    hasModule(ctx, USUARIOS_MODULE) &&
    hasPermission(ctx, USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.CRIAR)
  );
}

export function canEditUsuario(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
): boolean {
  return (
    hasModule(ctx, USUARIOS_MODULE) &&
    hasPermission(ctx, USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR)
  );
}

export function canToggleUsuarioStatus(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
  statusAtual: StatusUsuario,
): boolean {
  if (
    !hasModule(ctx, USUARIOS_MODULE) ||
    !hasPermission(ctx, USUARIOS_PERMISSIONS.ATUALIZAR)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  return statusAtual === "ATIVO" || statusAtual === "INATIVO";
}

export function canRunUsuarioAction(
  ctx: FrontendAuthorizationContext,
  action: UsuarioAction,
  statusAtual?: StatusUsuario,
): boolean {
  switch (action) {
    case "listar":
    case "ver":
      return canAccessUsuariosModule(ctx);
    case "criar":
      return canCreateUsuario(ctx);
    case "editar":
      return canEditUsuario(ctx);
    case "inativar":
    case "reativar":
      return statusAtual ? canToggleUsuarioStatus(ctx, statusAtual) : false;
    default:
      return false;
  }
}


===== apps/web/src/core/auth/authContext.test.tsx =====

// frontend/src/core/auth/__tests__/AuthContext.test.tsx

import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from "vitest";
import { render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import type { PropsWithChildren } from "react";

import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from "../AuthContext";

vi.mock("../../../features/auth/api/authApi", () => ({
  login: vi.fn(),
  logout: vi.fn(),
  me: vi.fn(),
  refresh: vi.fn(),
}));

vi.mock("../authStorage", () => ({
  authStorage: {
    getAccessToken: vi.fn(),
    setAccessToken: vi.fn(),
    removeAccessToken: vi.fn(),
    getRefreshToken: vi.fn(),
    setRefreshToken: vi.fn(),
    removeRefreshToken: vi.fn(),
    clear: vi.fn(),
  },
}));

import {
  login as loginRequest,
  me as meRequest,
  refresh as refreshRequest,
} from "../../../features/auth/api/authApi";
import { authStorage } from "../authStorage";
import { HttpError } from "../../api/httpClient";

function TestComponent(): JSX.Element {
  const { status, isAuthenticated, user } = useAuth();

  return (
    <div>
      <span data-testid="status">{status}</span>
      <span data-testid="is-authenticated">
        {isAuthenticated ? "true" : "false"}
      </span>
      <span data-testid="user-id">{user?.usuarioId ?? ""}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function Wrapper({ children }: PropsWithChildren): JSX.Element {
  return <AuthProvider>{children}</AuthProvider>;
}

describe("AuthContext", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it("deve iniciar como não autenticado quando não houver token", async () => {
    vi.mocked(authStorage.getAccessToken).mockReturnValue(null);

    render(<TestComponent />, { wrapper: Wrapper });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("status").textContent).toBe("unauthenticated");
      expect(screen.getByTestId("is-authenticated").textContent).toBe("false");
    });
  });

  it("deve autenticar sessão quando /auth/me retornar usuário", async () => {
    vi.mocked(authStorage.getAccessToken).mockReturnValue("token-valido");
    vi.mocked(meRequest).mockResolvedValue({
      usuario: {
        usuarioId: "u1",
        locatarioId: "l1",
        isSuperAdmin: false,
        permissoes: ["usuarios.ler"],
        papeis: ["ADMIN"],
        email: "user@example.com",
      },
    });

    render(<TestComponent />, { wrapper: Wrapper });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("status").textContent).toBe("authenticated");
      expect(screen.getByTestId("is-authenticated").textContent).toBe("true");
      expect(screen.getByTestId("user-id").textContent).toBe("u1");
    });
  });

  it("deve tentar refresh quando /auth/me retornar 401", async () => {
    vi.mocked(authStorage.getAccessToken).mockReturnValue("expired-token");
    vi.mocked(authStorage.getRefreshToken).mockReturnValue("refresh-token");

    vi.mocked(meRequest)
      .mockRejectedValueOnce(
        new HttpError({
          message: "Não autenticado",
          status: 401,
          code: "NAO_AUTENTICADO",
        }),
      )
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({
        usuario: {
          usuarioId: "u1",
          locatarioId: "l1",
          isSuperAdmin: false,
          permissoes: [],
          papeis: [],
        },
      });

    vi.mocked(refreshRequest).mockResolvedValue({
      accessToken: "new-access-token",
      refreshToken: "new-refresh-token",
    });

    render(<TestComponent />, { wrapper: Wrapper });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(refreshRequest).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(screen.getByTestId("status").textContent).toBe("authenticated");
      expect(screen.getByTestId("user-id").textContent).toBe("u1");
    });
  });

  it("deve limpar sessão quando refresh falhar", async () => {
    vi.mocked(authStorage.getAccessToken).mockReturnValue("expired-token");
    vi.mocked(authStorage.getRefreshToken).mockReturnValue("refresh-token");

    vi.mocked(meRequest).mockRejectedValue(
      new HttpError({
        message: "Não autenticado",
        status: 401,
        code: "NAO_AUTENTICADO",
      }),
    );

    vi.mocked(refreshRequest).mockRejectedValue(
      new Error("refresh falhou"),
    );

    render(<TestComponent />, { wrapper: Wrapper });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(authStorage.clear).toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(screen.getByTestId("status").textContent).toBe("unauthenticated");
    });
  });

  it("login deve armazenar tokens e autenticar usuário", async () => {
    function LoginCaller(): JSX.Element {
      const { login } = useAuth();

      return (
        <button
          onClick={() =>
            void login({
              email: "user@example.com",
              senha: "123456",
            })
          }
        >
          login
        </button>
      );
    }

    vi.mocked(authStorage.getAccessToken).mockReturnValue(null);

    vi.mocked(loginRequest).mockResolvedValue({
      accessToken: "access-token",
      refreshToken: "refresh-token",
      usuario: {
        usuarioId: "u1",
        locatarioId: "l1",
        isSuperAdmin: false,
        permissoes: [],
        papeis: [],
      },
    });

    render(
      <AuthProvider>
        <LoginCaller />
        <TestComponent />
      </AuthProvider>,
    );

    screen.getByRole("button", { name: /login/i }).click();

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(authStorage.setAccessToken).toHaveBeenCalledWith("access-token");
      expect(authStorage.setRefreshToken).toHaveBeenCalledWith("refresh-token");
      expect(screen.getByTestId("status").textContent).toBe("authenticated");
    });
  });
});

===== apps/web/src/core/auth/authContext.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/core/auth/authContext.tsx

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  type PropsWithChildren,
  type JSX,
} from "react";
import {
  registerAccessTokenGetter,
  registerRefreshSessionHandler,
  registerUnauthorizedHandler,
} from "../api/httpClient";
import { authStorage } from "./authStorage";
import {
  login as loginRequest,
  loginPlataforma as loginPlataformaRequest,
  logout as logoutRequest,
  logoutPlataforma as logoutPlataformaRequest,
  me as meRequest,
  refresh as refreshRequest,
  refreshPlataforma as refreshPlataformaRequest,
} from "../../features/auth/api/authApi";
import type {
  AuthContextValue,
  LoginInput,
  LoginPlataformaInput,
  SessionUser,
} from "./tipos";
import { setupInactivityWatcher } from "./sessionManager";

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | undefined>(
  undefined,
);

function normalizeUser(user: SessionUser): SessionUser {
  const perfil = user.perfil ?? (user.locatarioId ? "TENANT" : "PLATAFORMA");

  return {
    ...user,
    usuarioId: user.usuarioId,
    locatarioId: user.locatarioId ?? null,
    perfil,
  };
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }: PropsWithChildren): JSX.Element {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AuthContextValue["status"]>("idle");
  const [accessToken, setAccessToken] = useState<string | null>(
    authStorage.getAccessToken(),
  );
  const [user, setUser] = useState<SessionUser | null>(authStorage.getUser());

  const clearSession = useCallback(() => {
    authStorage.clear();
    setAccessToken(null);
    setUser(null);
    setStatus("unauthenticated");
  }, []);

  const applySession = useCallback(
    (params: {
      accessToken: string;
      refreshToken?: string;
      usuario: SessionUser;
    }): SessionUser => {
      const normalizedUser = normalizeUser(params.usuario);

      authStorage.setAccessToken(params.accessToken);
      authStorage.setUser(normalizedUser);
      authStorage.setLocatarioId(normalizedUser.locatarioId);

      if (params.refreshToken) {
        authStorage.setRefreshToken(params.refreshToken);
      } else {
        authStorage.removeRefreshToken();
      }

      setAccessToken(params.accessToken);
      setUser(normalizedUser);
      setStatus("authenticated");

      return normalizedUser;
    },
    [],
  );

  const carregarUsuarioAutenticado =
    useCallback(async (): Promise<SessionUser> => {
      const currentUser = authStorage.getUser();

      if (currentUser?.perfil === "PLATAFORMA") {
        const normalizedUser = normalizeUser(currentUser);

        authStorage.setUser(normalizedUser);
        authStorage.setLocatarioId(null);
        setUser(normalizedUser);

        return normalizedUser;
      }

      const meResponse = await meRequest();
      const normalizedUser = normalizeUser(meResponse.usuario);

      authStorage.setUser(normalizedUser);
      authStorage.setLocatarioId(normalizedUser.locatarioId);
      setUser(normalizedUser);

      return normalizedUser;
    }, []);

  const refreshTokens = useCallback(async (): Promise<boolean> => {
    const currentUser = authStorage.getUser();
    const refreshToken = authStorage.getRefreshToken();

    if (!currentUser || !refreshToken) {
      return false;
    }

    try {
      if (currentUser.perfil === "PLATAFORMA") {
        const response = await refreshPlataformaRequest({ refreshToken });

        authStorage.setAccessToken(response.accessToken);
        setAccessToken(response.accessToken);

        if (response.refreshToken) {
          authStorage.setRefreshToken(response.refreshToken);
        }

        return true;
      }

      const locatarioId =
        authStorage.getLocatarioId() ?? currentUser.locatarioId ?? null;

      if (!locatarioId) return false;

      const response = await refreshRequest({
        refreshToken,
        locatarioId,
      });

      authStorage.setAccessToken(response.accessToken);
      setAccessToken(response.accessToken);

      if (response.refreshToken) {
        authStorage.setRefreshToken(response.refreshToken);
      }

      return true;
    } catch {
      clearSession();
      return false;
    }
  }, [clearSession]);

  const login = useCallback(
    async (input: LoginInput): Promise<SessionUser> => {
      setStatus("loading");

      try {
        const response = await loginRequest(input);

        authStorage.setAccessToken(response.accessToken);
        setAccessToken(response.accessToken);

        if (response.refreshToken) {
          authStorage.setRefreshToken(response.refreshToken);
        } else {
          authStorage.removeRefreshToken();
        }

        const usuario =
          (response as { usuario?: SessionUser }).usuario ??
          (await meRequest()).usuario;

        return applySession({
          accessToken: response.accessToken,
          refreshToken: response.refreshToken,
          usuario,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        clearSession();
        throw error;
      }
    },
    [applySession, clearSession],
  );

  const loginPlataforma = useCallback(
    async (input: LoginPlataformaInput): Promise<SessionUser> => {
      setStatus("loading");

      try {
        const response = await loginPlataformaRequest(input);

        return applySession({
          accessToken: response.accessToken,
          refreshToken: response.refreshToken,
          usuario: response.usuario,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        clearSession();
        throw error;
      }
    },
    [applySession, clearSession],
  );

  const logout = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {
    const refreshToken = authStorage.getRefreshToken();
    const locatarioId = authStorage.getLocatarioId();
    const currentUser = authStorage.getUser() ?? user;

    try {
      if (refreshToken && currentUser?.perfil === "PLATAFORMA") {
        await logoutPlataformaRequest({ refreshToken });
        return;
      }

      if (refreshToken && currentUser?.perfil === "TENANT") {
        await logoutRequest({
          refreshToken,
          locatarioId,
        });
      }
    } finally {
      clearSession();
    }
  }, [clearSession, user]);

  useEffect(() => {
    registerAccessTokenGetter(() => authStorage.getAccessToken());
    registerRefreshSessionHandler(refreshTokens);
    registerUnauthorizedHandler(clearSession);

    return () => {
      registerAccessTokenGetter(() => null);
      registerRefreshSessionHandler(null);
      registerUnauthorizedHandler(null);
    };
  }, [clearSession, refreshTokens]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let ativo = true;

    async function bootstrap() {
      const token = authStorage.getAccessToken();

      if (!token) {
        if (ativo) setStatus("unauthenticated");
        return;
      }

      setStatus("loading");

      try {
        await carregarUsuarioAutenticado();

        if (ativo) setStatus("authenticated");
      } catch {
        if (ativo) clearSession();
      }
    }

    void bootstrap();

    return () => {
      ativo = false;
    };
  }, [carregarUsuarioAutenticado, clearSession]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (status !== "authenticated" || !user) return;

    return setupInactivityWatcher({
      user,
      onExpired: logout,
    });
  }, [logout, status, user]);

  const value = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(
    () => ({
      status,
      user,
      accessToken,
      isLoading: status === "idle" || status === "loading",
      isAuthenticated: status === "authenticated" && Boolean(user),
      login,
      loginPlataforma,
      logout,
      refreshTokens,
    }),
    [
      status,
      user,
      accessToken,
      login,
      loginPlataforma,
      logout,
      refreshTokens,
    ],
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}


===== apps/web/src/core/auth/authStorage.ts =====

// apps/web/src/core/auth/authStorage.ts

import type { SessionUser } from "./tipos";

const ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY = "app.access_token";
const REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY = "app.refresh_token";
const LOCATARIO_ID_KEY = "app.locatario_id";
const USER_KEY = "app.auth_user";

function safeGet(key: string): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(key);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

function safeSet(key: string, value: string): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, value);
  } catch {
    // storage não deve quebrar a aplicação
  }
}

function safeRemove(key: string): void {
  try {
    localStorage.removeItem(key);
  } catch {
    // noop
  }
}

export const authStorage = {
  getAccessToken(): string | null {
    return safeGet(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY);
  },

  setAccessToken(token: string): void {
    safeSet(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY, token);
  },

  removeAccessToken(): void {
    safeRemove(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY);
  },

  getRefreshToken(): string | null {
    return safeGet(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY);
  },

  setRefreshToken(token: string): void {
    safeSet(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
  },

  removeRefreshToken(): void {
    safeRemove(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY);
  },

  getLocatarioId(): string | null {
    return safeGet(LOCATARIO_ID_KEY);
  },

  setLocatarioId(locatarioId: string | null): void {
    if (locatarioId) {
      safeSet(LOCATARIO_ID_KEY, locatarioId);
    } else {
      safeRemove(LOCATARIO_ID_KEY);
    }
  },

  getUser(): SessionUser | null {
    const raw = safeGet(USER_KEY);

    if (!raw) return null;

    try {
      return JSON.parse(raw) as SessionUser;
    } catch {
      safeRemove(USER_KEY);
      return null;
    }
  },

  setUser(user: SessionUser): void {
    safeSet(USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
  },

  removeUser(): void {
    safeRemove(USER_KEY);
  },

  clear(): void {
    safeRemove(ACCESS_TOKEN_KEY);
    safeRemove(REFRESH_TOKEN_KEY);
    safeRemove(LOCATARIO_ID_KEY);
    safeRemove(USER_KEY);
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/core/auth/sessionManager.ts =====

// apps/web/src/core/auth/sessionManager.ts

import type { SessionUser } from "./tipos";

export const DEFAULT_TENANT_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000;
export const DEFAULT_PLATAFORMA_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS = 20 * 60 * 1000;

const ACTIVITY_EVENTS: Array<keyof WindowEventMap> = [
  "click",
  "keydown",
  "mousemove",
  "mousedown",
  "scroll",
  "touchstart",
];

function readTimeoutEnv(
  key: "VITE_TENANT_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS" | "VITE_PLATAFORMA_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS",
  fallback: number,
): number {
  const raw = import.meta.env[key];
  if (!raw) return fallback;

  const value = Number(raw);
  return Number.isFinite(value) && value > 0 ? value : fallback;
}

export function getIdleTimeoutMs(user: SessionUser | null): number {
  if (user?.perfil === "PLATAFORMA") {
    return readTimeoutEnv(
      "VITE_PLATAFORMA_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS",
      DEFAULT_PLATAFORMA_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS,
    );
  }

  return readTimeoutEnv(
    "VITE_TENANT_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS",
    DEFAULT_TENANT_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS,
  );
}

export type InactivityWatcherParams = {
  user: SessionUser;
  onExpired: () => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function setupInactivityWatcher({
  user,
  onExpired,
}: InactivityWatcherParams): () => void {
  const timeoutMs = getIdleTimeoutMs(user);

  let timeoutId: number | undefined;
  let expired = false;

  async function expireSession() {
    if (expired) return;
    expired = true;
    await onExpired();
  }

  function resetTimer() {
    if (expired) return;

    if (timeoutId !== undefined) {
      window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }

    timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
      void expireSession();
    }, timeoutMs);
  }

  for (const eventName of ACTIVITY_EVENTS) {
    window.addEventListener(eventName, resetTimer, { passive: true });
  }

  resetTimer();

  return () => {
    if (timeoutId !== undefined) {
      window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }

    for (const eventName of ACTIVITY_EVENTS) {
      window.removeEventListener(eventName, resetTimer);
    }
  };
}


===== apps/web/src/core/auth/tipos.ts =====

// apps/web/src/core/auth/tipos.ts

export type PerfilSessao = "TENANT" | "PLATAFORMA";

export type SessionUser = {
  usuarioId: string;
  locatarioId: string | null;
  perfil: PerfilSessao;
  email?: string;
  nome?: string;
  papeis?: string[];
  permissoes?: string[];
  modulosHabilitados?: string[];
};

export type LoginInput = {
  email: string;
  senha: string;
  locatarioSlug: string;
};

export type LoginPlataformaInput = {
  email: string;
  senha: string;
};

export type SessaoResponse = {
  accessToken: string;
  accessExpiraEm: string;
  refreshToken?: string;
  refreshExpiraEm?: string;
  usuario: SessionUser;
};

export type LoginResponse = SessaoResponse;

export type LoginPlataformaResponse = SessaoResponse;

export type RefreshInput = {
  refreshToken: string;
  locatarioId: string | null;
};

export type RefreshResponse = {
  accessToken: string;
  accessExpiraEm?: string;
  refreshToken?: string;
  refreshExpiraEm?: string;
};

export type MeResponse = {
  usuario: SessionUser;
};

export type LogoutInput = {
  locatarioId: string | null;
  refreshToken: string;
};

export type AuthStatus =
  | "idle"
  | "loading"
  | "authenticated"
  | "unauthenticated";

export type AuthContextValue = {
  status: AuthStatus;
  user: SessionUser | null;
  accessToken: string | null;
  isLoading: boolean;
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  login: (input: LoginInput) => Promise<SessionUser>;
  loginPlataforma: (input: LoginPlataformaInput) => Promise<SessionUser>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  refreshTokens: () => Promise<boolean>;
};

export function isTenant(user: SessionUser | null | undefined): boolean {
  return user?.perfil === "TENANT";
}

export function isPlataforma(user: SessionUser | null | undefined): boolean {
  return user?.perfil === "PLATAFORMA";
}

export function isSuperAdmin(user: SessionUser | null | undefined): boolean {
  return isPlataforma(user);
}

===== apps/web/src/core/auth/useAuthorization.ts =====

// apps/web/src/core/auth/useAuthorization.ts

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { canAccessUsuariosModule, canCreateUsuario, canEditUsuario, hasModule, hasPermission, type FrontendAuthorizationContext } from "../api/permissions";
import { useAuth } from "./useAuth";
import { isSuperAdmin } from "./tipos";

export function useAuthorization() {
  const { user, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();

  const ctx = useMemo<FrontendAuthorizationContext>(
    () => ({
      permissions: user?.permissoes ?? [],
      modules: user?.modulosHabilitados ?? [],
      isSuperAdmin: isSuperAdmin(user),
    }),
    [user],
  );

  return {
    isAuthenticated,
    user,
    ctx,
    permissions: ctx.permissions ?? [],
    modules: ctx.modules ?? [],
    isSuperAdmin: ctx.isSuperAdmin === true,
    hasPermission: (permission: string) => hasPermission(ctx, permission),
    hasModule: (moduleCode: string) => hasModule(ctx, moduleCode),
    canAccessUsuariosModule: () => canAccessUsuariosModule(ctx),
    canCreateUsuario: () => canCreateUsuario(ctx),
    canEditUsuario: () => canEditUsuario(ctx),
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/core/auth/useAuth.ts =====

// apps/web/src/core/auth/useAuth.ts

import { useContext } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "./authContext";
import type { AuthContextValue } from "./tipos";

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!ctx) {
    throw new Error("useAuth deve ser usado dentro de AuthProvider.");
  }
  return ctx;
}

export type AppErrorCode =
  | "NAO_AUTORIZADO"
  | "NAO_AUTENTICADO"
  | "INVALIDO"
  | "NAO_ENCONTRADO"
  | "ERRO_INTERNO"

===== apps/web/src/core/errors/mapError.test.ts =====

// frontend/src/core/errors/__tests__/mapError.test.ts

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import { mapError } from "../mapError";
import { HttpError } from "../../api/httpClient";

describe("mapError", () => {
  it("deve mapear NAO_AUTENTICADO", () => {
    const error = new HttpError({
      message: "Sessão expirada",
      status: 401,
      code: "NAO_AUTENTICADO",
    });

    const result = mapError(error);

    expect(result.code).toBe("NAO_AUTENTICADO");
    expect(result.message).toBe("Sessão expirada");
  });

  it("deve mapear 403 sem código explícito", () => {
    const error = new HttpError({
      message: "Forbidden",
      status: 403,
    });

    const result = mapError(error);

    expect(result.code).toBe("NAO_AUTORIZADO");
  });

  it("deve mapear erro comum para UNKNOWN", () => {
    const result = mapError(new Error("Falha inesperada"));

    expect(result.code).toBe("UNKNOWN");
    expect(result.message).toBe("Falha inesperada");
  });
});

===== apps/web/src/core/errors/mapError.ts =====

// frontend/src/core/errors/mapError.ts

import { HttpError } from "../api/httpClient";
import type { AppErrorCode } from "./errorCodes";

export type UiError = {
  code: AppErrorCode | "UNKNOWN";
  title: string;
  message: string;
  status?: number;
};

function buildDefaultMessageByCode(code: AppErrorCode): UiError {
  switch (code) {
    case "NAO_AUTENTICADO":
      return {
        code,
        title: "Sessão inválida",
        message: "Sua sessão expirou ou é inválida. Faça login novamente.",
      };

    case "NAO_AUTORIZADO":
      return {
        code,
        title: "Acesso negado",
        message: "Você não possui permissão para realizar esta ação.",
      };

    case "INVALIDO":
      return {
        code,
        title: "Dados inválidos",
        message: "Verifique os dados informados e tente novamente.",
      };

    case "NAO_ENCONTRADO":
      return {
        code,
        title: "Não encontrado",
        message: "O recurso solicitado não foi encontrado.",
      };

    case "CONFLITO":
      return {
        code,
        title: "Conflito",
        message: "Não foi possível concluir a operação por conflito de dados.",
      };

    case "ERRO_INTERNO":
      return {
        code,
        title: "Erro interno",
        message: "Ocorreu um erro interno. Tente novamente mais tarde.",
      };
    default:
      return {
        code: "UNKNOWN",
        title: "Erro",
        message: "Ocorreu um erro inesperado.",
      };
  }
}

export function mapError(error: unknown): UiError {
  if (error instanceof HttpError) {
    const code = error.code as AppErrorCode | undefined;

    if (code) {
      const mapped = buildDefaultMessageByCode(code);

      return {
        ...mapped,
        status: error.status,
        message: error.message?.trim() || mapped.message,
      };
    }

    if (error.status === 401) {
      return {
        code: "NAO_AUTENTICADO",
        title: "Sessão inválida",
        message: "Sua sessão expirou ou é inválida. Faça login novamente.",
        status: error.status,
      };
    }

    if (error.status === 403) {
      return {
        code: "NAO_AUTORIZADO",
        title: "Acesso negado",
        message: "Você não possui permissão para acessar este recurso.",
        status: error.status,
      };
    }

    if (error.status === 404) {
      return {
        code: "NAO_ENCONTRADO",
        title: "Não encontrado",
        message: "O recurso solicitado não foi encontrado.",
        status: error.status,
      };
    }

    if (error.status === 409) {
      return {
        code: "CONFLITO",
        title: "Conflito",
        message: "Não foi possível concluir a operação por conflito de dados.",
        status: error.status,
      };
    }

    if (error.status >= 400 && error.status < 500) {
      return {
        code: "INVALIDO",
        title: "Solicitação inválida",
        message: error.message || "A solicitação não pôde ser processada.",
        status: error.status,
      };
    }

    return {
      code: "ERRO_INTERNO",
      title: "Erro interno",
      message: error.message || "Ocorreu um erro interno.",
      status: error.status,
    };
  }

  if (error instanceof Error) {
    return {
      code: "UNKNOWN",
      title: "Erro inesperado",
      message: error.message || "Ocorreu um erro inesperado.",
    };
  }

  return {
    code: "UNKNOWN",
    title: "Erro inesperado",
    message: "Ocorreu um erro inesperado.",
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/core/routing/pageGuard.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/core/routing/pageGuard.tsx

import type { PropsWithChildren, ReactNode } from "react";
import { useAuthorization } from "../auth/useAuthorization";

type PageGuardProps = PropsWithChildren<{
  requiredPermissions?: string[];
  requiredModule?: string;
  requireAllPermissions?: boolean;
  fallback?: ReactNode;
}>;

function hasRequiredPermissions(currentPermissions: string[], requiredPermissions: string[], requireAllPermissions: boolean): boolean {
  if (requiredPermissions.length === 0) {
    return true;
  }

  if (requireAllPermissions) {
    return requiredPermissions.every((permission) =>
      currentPermissions.includes(permission),
    );
  }

  return requiredPermissions.some((permission) =>
    currentPermissions.includes(permission),
  );
}

export function PageGuard({ children, requiredPermissions = [], requiredModule, requireAllPermissions = true, fallback }: PageGuardProps) {
  const { hasModule, permissions } = useAuthorization();

  if (requiredModule && !hasModule(requiredModule)) {
    return (
      fallback ?? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-800">
          Você não possui acesso a este módulo.
        </div>
      )
    );
  }

  const ok = hasRequiredPermissions(
    permissions,
    requiredPermissions,
    requireAllPermissions,
  );

  if (!ok) {
    return (
      fallback ?? (
        <div className="rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-800">
          Você não possui permissão para acessar este conteúdo.
        </div>
      )
    );
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

===== apps/web/src/core/routing/protectedRoute.tsx =====

// frontend/src/core/routing/ProtectedRoute.tsx

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../auth/useAuth";

type ProtectedRouteProps = {
  redirectTo?: string;
};

export function ProtectedRoute({
  redirectTo = "/login",
}: ProtectedRouteProps) {
  const location = useLocation();
  const { isLoading, isAuthenticated } = useAuth();

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div>Carregando sessão...</div>;
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated) {
    return (
      <Navigate
        to={redirectTo}
        replace
        state={{ from: location }}
      />
    );
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

export default ProtectedRoute;


===== apps/web/src/core/routing/superAdminRoute.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/core/routing/superAdminRoute.tsx

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../auth/useAuth";
import { isPlataforma } from "../auth/tipos";
import { JSX } from "react";

type SuperAdminRouteProps = {
  loginPath?: string;
  forbiddenPath?: string;
};

export function SuperAdminRoute({
  loginPath = "/login",
  forbiddenPath = "/app",
}: SuperAdminRouteProps): JSX.Element {
  const location = useLocation();
  const { isLoading, isAuthenticated, user } = useAuth();

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div>Carregando sessão...</div>;
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated || !user) {
    return (
      <Navigate
        to={loginPath}
        replace
        state={{ from: location }}
      />
    );
  }

  if (!isPlataforma(user)) {
    return <Navigate to={forbiddenPath} replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

export default SuperAdminRoute;

===== apps/web/src/core/routing/tenantRoute.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/core/routing/tenantRoute.tsx

import { Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
import { useAuth } from "../auth/useAuth";
import { JSX } from "react";

type TenantRouteProps = {
  loginPath?: string;
  forbiddenPath?: string;
};

function isTenantLike(user: unknown): boolean {
  if (!user || typeof user !== "object") return false;

  const perfil = (user as { perfil?: string }).perfil;
  const locatarioId = (user as { locatarioId?: string | null }).locatarioId;

  return perfil === "TENANT" || Boolean(locatarioId);
}

export function TenantRoute({
  loginPath = "/login",
  forbiddenPath = "/acesso-negado",
}: TenantRouteProps): JSX.Element {
  const location = useLocation();
  const { isLoading, isAuthenticated, user } = useAuth();

  if (isLoading) {
    return <div>Carregando sessão...</div>;
  }

  if (!isAuthenticated || !user) {
    return (
      <Navigate
        to={loginPath}
        replace
        state={{ from: location }}
      />
    );
  }

  if (!isTenantLike(user)) {
    return <Navigate to={forbiddenPath} replace />;
  }

  return <Outlet />;
}

export default TenantRoute;

===== apps/web/src/core/tenant/tenantBranding.ts =====

// apps/web/src/core/tenant/tenantBranding.ts

import type {
  ConfiguracaoAtivaLocatarioDto,
  TenantBranding,
} from "./tipos";

const DEFAULT_PRIMARY = "#2563eb";
const DEFAULT_SECONDARY = "#1d4ed8";
const DEFAULT_BACKGROUND = "#f8fafc";
const DEFAULT_TEXT = "#111827";

function limparOpcional(value?: string | null): string | undefined {
  const trimmed = value?.trim();
  return trimmed ? trimmed : undefined;
}

export function buildFallbackTenantBranding(
  locatarioId: string,
): TenantBranding {
  return {
    locatarioId,
    nomeFantasia: `Locatário ${locatarioId.slice(0, 8)}`,
    logoUrl: undefined,
    corPrimaria: DEFAULT_PRIMARY,
    corSecundaria: DEFAULT_SECONDARY,
    corFundo: DEFAULT_BACKGROUND,
    corTexto: DEFAULT_TEXT,
    modoTema: "CLARO",
  };
}

export function mapConfiguracaoAtivaToTenantBranding(
  configuracao: ConfiguracaoAtivaLocatarioDto,
): TenantBranding {
  return {
    locatarioId: configuracao.locatarioId,
    configuracaoId: configuracao.configuracaoId,
    nomeFantasia: limparOpcional(configuracao.identidadeVisual) || `Locatário ${configuracao.locatarioId.slice(0, 8)}`,
    logoUrl: limparOpcional(configuracao.urlLogo),
    corPrimaria: limparOpcional(configuracao.corPrimaria) || DEFAULT_PRIMARY,
    corSecundaria: limparOpcional(configuracao.corSecundaria) || DEFAULT_SECONDARY,
    corFundo: DEFAULT_BACKGROUND,
    corTexto: limparOpcional(configuracao.corTerciaria) || DEFAULT_TEXT,
    modoTema: "CLARO",
  };
}

export function normalizeTenantBranding(
  branding: TenantBranding,
): TenantBranding {
  return {
    locatarioId: branding.locatarioId,
    configuracaoId: branding.configuracaoId,
    nomeFantasia:
      limparOpcional(branding.nomeFantasia) ||
      `Locatário ${branding.locatarioId.slice(0, 8)}`,
    logoUrl: limparOpcional(branding.logoUrl),
    corPrimaria: limparOpcional(branding.corPrimaria) || DEFAULT_PRIMARY,
    corSecundaria: limparOpcional(branding.corSecundaria) || DEFAULT_SECONDARY,
    corFundo: DEFAULT_BACKGROUND,
    corTexto: limparOpcional(branding.corTexto) || DEFAULT_TEXT,
    modoTema: "CLARO",
  };
}


===== apps/web/src/core/tenant/tenantContext.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/core/tenant/tenantContext.tsx

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  type PropsWithChildren,
} from "react";

import { HttpError } from "../api/httpClient";
import { useAuth } from "../auth/useAuth";
import { isSuperAdmin } from "../auth/tipos";
import { getTenantConfiguracaoAtiva } from "./tenantApi";
import {
  buildFallbackTenantBranding,
  mapConfiguracaoAtivaToTenantBranding,
  normalizeTenantBranding,
} from "./tenantBranding";
import type { TenantBranding, TenantContextValue } from "./tipos";

const TenantContext = createContext<TenantContextValue | undefined>(undefined);

export function TenantProvider({ children }: PropsWithChildren) {
  const { user, isAuthenticated, isLoading: isAuthLoading } = useAuth();

  const [branding, setBranding] = useState<TenantBranding | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);

  const refreshBranding = useCallback(async () => {
    if (
      !isAuthenticated ||
      !user ||
      isSuperAdmin(user) ||
      !user.locatarioId
    ) {
      setBranding(null);
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);

    try {
      const response = await getTenantConfiguracaoAtiva();

      setBranding(
        normalizeTenantBranding(
          mapConfiguracaoAtivaToTenantBranding(response.item),
        ),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof HttpError) {
        setBranding(buildFallbackTenantBranding(user.locatarioId));
        return;
      }

      setBranding(buildFallbackTenantBranding(user.locatarioId));
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }, [isAuthenticated, user]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAuthLoading) return;

    void refreshBranding();
  }, [isAuthLoading, refreshBranding]);

  const value = useMemo<TenantContextValue>(
    () => ({
      branding,
      isLoading,
      refreshBranding,
    }),
    [branding, isLoading, refreshBranding],
  );

  return (
    <TenantContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </TenantContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTenant(): TenantContextValue {
  const context = useContext(TenantContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useTenant deve ser usado dentro de TenantProvider");
  }

  return context;
}


===== apps/web/src/core/tenant/tenantThemeProvider.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/core/tenant/tenantThemeProvider.tsx

import { useEffect, type PropsWithChildren } from "react";
import { useTenant } from "./tenantContext";

const DEFAULT_THEME = {
  primary: "#2563eb",
  secondary: "#1d4ed8",
  background: "#f8fafc",
  text: "#111827",
};

export function TenantThemeProvider({ children }: PropsWithChildren) {
  const { branding } = useTenant();

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;

    const primary = branding?.corPrimaria ?? DEFAULT_THEME.primary;
    const secondary = branding?.corSecundaria ?? DEFAULT_THEME.secondary;
    const background = branding?.corFundo ?? DEFAULT_THEME.background;
    const text = branding?.corTexto ?? DEFAULT_THEME.text;

    root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-primary", primary);
    root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-secondary", secondary);
    root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-background", background);
    root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-text", text);

    root.style.setProperty("--ck-primary", primary);
    root.style.setProperty("--ck-secondary", secondary);
    root.style.setProperty("--ck-background", background);
    root.style.setProperty("--ck-text", text);

    return () => {
      root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-primary", DEFAULT_THEME.primary);
      root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-secondary", DEFAULT_THEME.secondary);
      root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-background", DEFAULT_THEME.background);
      root.style.setProperty("--tenant-color-text", DEFAULT_THEME.text);

      root.style.setProperty("--ck-primary", DEFAULT_THEME.primary);
      root.style.setProperty("--ck-secondary", DEFAULT_THEME.secondary);
      root.style.setProperty("--ck-background", DEFAULT_THEME.background);
      root.style.setProperty("--ck-text", DEFAULT_THEME.text);
    };
  }, [branding]);

  return <>{children}</>;
}

===== apps/web/src/features/auth/api/authApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/auth/api/authApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../core/api/httpClient";
import {
  LoginInput,
  LoginPlataformaInput,
  LoginPlataformaResponse,
  LoginResponse,
  LogoutInput,
  MeResponse,
  RefreshInput,
  RefreshResponse,
} from "../../../core/auth/tipos";

export async function login(input: LoginInput): Promise<LoginResponse> {
  return httpClient.post<LoginResponse, LoginInput>("/auth/login", input);
}

export async function loginPlataforma(
  input: LoginPlataformaInput,
): Promise<LoginPlataformaResponse> {
  return httpClient.post<LoginPlataformaResponse, LoginPlataformaInput>(
    "/auth/plataforma/login",
    input,
    { skipAuthRetry: true },
  );
}

export async function me(): Promise<MeResponse> {
  return httpClient.get<MeResponse>("/auth/me", { skipAuthRetry: true });
}

export async function refresh(input: RefreshInput): Promise<RefreshResponse> {
  return httpClient.post<RefreshResponse, RefreshInput>(
    "/auth/refresh",
    input,
    {
      headers: { Authorization: "" },
      skipAuthRetry: true,
    },
  );
}

export async function refreshPlataforma(input: {
  refreshToken: string;
}): Promise<RefreshResponse> {
  return httpClient.post<RefreshResponse, { refreshToken: string }>(
    "/auth/plataforma/refresh",
    input,
    {
      headers: { Authorization: "" },
      skipAuthRetry: true,
    },
  );
}

export async function logout(input: LogoutInput): Promise<void> {
  return httpClient.post<void, LogoutInput>("/auth/logout", input, {
    headers: { Authorization: "" },
    skipAuthRetry: true,
  });
}

export async function logoutPlataforma(input: {
  refreshToken: string;
}): Promise<void> {
  return httpClient.post<void, { refreshToken: string }>(
    "/auth/plataforma/logout",
    input,
    {
      headers: { Authorization: "" },
      skipAuthRetry: true,
    },
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/auth/pages/LoginPage.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/auth/pages/LoginPage.tsx

import type { JSX } from "react";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useAuth } from "../../../core/auth/useAuth";
import "./login.css";

export default function LoginPage(): JSX.Element {
  const { login, isLoading } = useAuth();

  const [locatarioSlug, setLocatarioSlug] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [senha, setSenha] = useState("");
  const [erro, setErro] = useState<string | null>(null);

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setErro(null);

    try {
      await login({
        locatarioSlug: locatarioSlug.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        senha,
      });
    } catch (error) {
      setErro(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Erro ao autenticar.");
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="ck-login-shell">
      <form className="ck-login-card ck-login-tenant" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
        <header className="ck-login-header">
          <div className="ck-login-header-content">
            <div className="ck-login-mark">CK</div>
            <span className="ck-login-badge">Beta • Portfólio</span>
            <h1 className="ck-login-title">Entrar no Core-kit</h1>
            <p className="ck-login-subtitle">
              Sistema de gestão para clientes, produtos, compras e vendas.
            </p>
          </div>
        </header>

        <section className="ck-login-body">
          {erro ? <div className="ck-login-error">{erro}</div> : null}

          <label className="ck-login-field">
            <span className="ck-login-label">Locatário</span>
            <input
              className="ck-login-input"
              placeholder="Ex.: loja"
              value={locatarioSlug}
              onChange={(event) => setLocatarioSlug(event.target.value)}
              autoComplete="organization"
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="ck-login-field">
            <span className="ck-login-label">E-mail</span>
            <input
              className="ck-login-input"
              placeholder="usuario@email.com.br"
              type="email"
              value={email}
              onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
              autoComplete="email"
              required
            />
          </label>

          <label className="ck-login-field">
            <span className="ck-login-label">Senha</span>
            <input
              className="ck-login-input"
              placeholder="Sua senha"
              type="password"
              value={senha}
              onChange={(event) => setSenha(event.target.value)}
              autoComplete="current-password"
              required
            />
          </label>

          <button className="ck-login-submit" type="submit" disabled={isLoading}>
            {isLoading ? "Entrando..." : "Entrar"}
          </button>

          <p className="ck-login-footer">
            <strong>Core-kit Beta</strong> • Demonstração de portfólio
          </p>
        </section>
      </form>
    </main>
  );
}

===== apps/web/src/features/dashboard/components/DashboardCard.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/dashboard/components/DashboardCard.tsx

import type { JSX, ReactNode } from "react";
import "./dashboardCard.css";

type DashboardCardVariant =
  | "default"
  | "success"
  | "warning"
  | "danger"
  | "info"
  | "purple";

type DashboardCardProps = {
  title: string;
  value: ReactNode;
  icon?: ReactNode;
  description?: ReactNode;
  footer?: ReactNode;
  variant?: DashboardCardVariant;
  isLoading?: boolean;
};

function variantColor(variant: DashboardCardVariant): string {
  switch (variant) {
    case "success":
      return "#16a34a";
    case "warning":
      return "#f59e0b";
    case "danger":
      return "#dc2626";
    case "info":
      return "#2563eb";
    case "purple":
      return "#7c3aed";
    case "default":
    default:
      return "var(--ck-primary, #2563eb)";
  }
}

export function DashboardCard({
  title,
  value,
  icon,
  description,
  footer,
  variant = "default",
  isLoading = false,
}: DashboardCardProps): JSX.Element {
  const color = variantColor(variant);

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <article className="ck-dashboard-card ck-dashboard-card-loading">
        <div className="ck-dashboard-card-skeleton" />
      </article>
    );
  }

  return (
    <article
      className="ck-dashboard-card"
      style={{ "--dashboard-card-color": color } as React.CSSProperties}
    >
      <div className="ck-dashboard-card-top">
        <div>
          <div className="ck-dashboard-card-title">{title}</div>
          <div className="ck-dashboard-card-value">{value}</div>

          {description ? (
            <div className="ck-dashboard-card-description">{description}</div>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        {icon ? <div className="ck-dashboard-card-icon">{icon}</div> : null}
      </div>

      {footer ? <div className="ck-dashboard-card-footer">{footer}</div> : null}
    </article>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/dashboard/components/index.ts =====

===== apps/web/src/features/dashboard/hooks/useDashboardResumo.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/dashboard/hooks/useDashboardResumo.ts

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { DashboardResumo } from "../tipos";
import { obterDashboardResumo } from "../api/dashboardApi";

export function useDashboardResumo() {
  const [item, setItem] = useState<DashboardResumo | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    obterDashboardResumo()
      .then((response) => setItem(response.item))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  return {
    item,
    loading,
  };
}

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../../core/api/httpClient";

export type BrandingLocatario = {
  locatarioId: string;
  configuracaoId: string;
  modoTema?: string | null;
  identidadeVisual?: string | null;
  corPrimaria?: string | null;
  corSecundaria?: string | null;
  corTerciaria?: string | null;
  urlLogo?: string | null;
  ativa?: boolean;
  atualizadoEm?: string | null;
};

export type AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput = {
  nomeFantasia?: string | null;
  corPrimaria?: string | null;
  corSecundaria?: string | null;
  corFundo?: string | null;
  corTexto?: string | null;
  logoUrl?: string | null;
};

export type ModuloAssinaturaLocatario = {
  codigo: string;
  origem?: string | null;
};

export type ObterBrandingLocatarioResponse = { item: BrandingLocatario };
export type AtualizarBrandingLocatarioResponse = { item: BrandingLocatario };
export type ListarModulosAssinaturaLocatarioResponse = {
  locatarioId: string;
  modulos: ModuloAssinaturaLocatario[];
};
export type VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioInput = { codigo: string };
export type VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse = {
  item: ModuloAssinaturaLocatario;
};
export type DesvincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse = {
  item: { codigo: string };
};

export const locatarioConfiguracoesApi = {
  obterBranding(locatarioId: string): Promise<ObterBrandingLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ObterBrandingLocatarioResponse>(
      `/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/configuracoes/ativa`,
    );
  },

  atualizarBranding(locatarioId: string, input: AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput): Promise<AtualizarBrandingLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.patch<
      AtualizarBrandingLocatarioResponse,
      AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput
    >(`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/branding`, input);
  },

  listarModulos(locatarioId: string): Promise<ListarModulosAssinaturaLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ListarModulosAssinaturaLocatarioResponse>(
      `/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/modulos`,
    );
  },

  vincularModulo(locatarioId: string, input: VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioInput): Promise<VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<
      VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse,
      VincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioInput
    >(`/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/modulos`, input);
  },

  desvincularModulo(locatarioId: string, codigo: string): Promise<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.delete<DesvincularModuloAssinaturaLocatarioResponse>(
      `/plataforma/locatarios/${locatarioId}/modulos/${encodeURIComponent(codigo)}`,
    );
  },
};


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatariosApi.ts =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/api/locatariosApi.ts

import { httpClient } from "../../../../core/api/httpClient";
import { AtualizarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput, CriarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput } from "../../../tenant-admin/locatario";
import type { AcaoLocatarioResponse, AtualizarLocatarioInput, AtualizarLocatarioResponse, CriarLocatarioInput, CriarLocatarioResponse, EncerrarLocatarioInput,
  ListarLocatariosResponse, ObterLocatarioResponse, ReativarLocatarioInput, SuspenderLocatarioInput } from "../tipos";

const BASE_PLATAFORMA = "/plataforma/locatarios";
const BASE = "/locatarios";
const BASE_ME = "/me/locatario";

export const locatariosApi = {
  listar(): Promise<ListarLocatariosResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ListarLocatariosResponse>(BASE_PLATAFORMA);
  },

  obter(locatarioId: string): Promise<ObterLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.get<ObterLocatarioResponse>(`${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}`);
  },

  criar(input: CriarLocatarioInput): Promise<CriarLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<CriarLocatarioResponse, CriarLocatarioInput>(
      BASE_PLATAFORMA,
      input,
    );
  },

  atualizar(locatarioId: string, input: AtualizarLocatarioInput): Promise<AtualizarLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.patch<AtualizarLocatarioResponse, AtualizarLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}`,
      input,
    );
  },

  suspender(locatarioId: string, input: SuspenderLocatarioInput = {}): Promise<AcaoLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<AcaoLocatarioResponse, SuspenderLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}/suspender`,
      input,
    );
  },

  reativar(locatarioId: string, input: ReativarLocatarioInput = {}): Promise<AcaoLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<AcaoLocatarioResponse, ReativarLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}/reativar`,
      input,
    );
  },

  encerrar(locatarioId: string, input: EncerrarLocatarioInput = {}): Promise<AcaoLocatarioResponse> {
    return httpClient.post<AcaoLocatarioResponse, EncerrarLocatarioInput>(
      `${BASE_PLATAFORMA}/${locatarioId}/encerrar`,
      input,
    );
  },

  // próprio locatário
  obterMeu(): Promise<ObterLocatarioResponse> {
    // o backend ignora o :id e usa ctx.locatarioId
    return httpClient.get(`${BASE}/me`);
  },

  // 👇 CONFIGURAÇÕES (NOVO CONTRATO)
  listarConfiguracoes() {
    return httpClient.get(`${BASE_ME}/configuracoes`);
  },

  obterConfiguracaoAtiva() {
    return httpClient.get(`${BASE_ME}/configuracoes/ativa`);
  },

  criarConfiguracao(input: CriarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput) {
    return httpClient.post(`${BASE_ME}/configuracoes`, input);
  },

  atualizarConfiguracao(configuracaoId: string, input: AtualizarConfiguracaoLocatarioInput) {
    return httpClient.patch(
      `${BASE_ME}/configuracoes/${configuracaoId}`,
      input,
    );
  },

  ativarConfiguracao(configuracaoId: string) {
    return httpClient.post(
      `${BASE_ME}/configuracoes/${configuracaoId}/ativar`,
    );
  },
};

===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingForm.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingForm.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput, BrandingLocatario } from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

type Props = {
  branding: BrandingLocatario | null;
  isSaving: boolean;
  onSubmit: (input: AtualizarBrandingLocatarioInput) => void | Promise<void>;
};

export function LocatarioBrandingForm({ branding, isSaving, onSubmit }: Props) {
  const [nomeFantasia, setNomeFantasia] = useState("");
  const [logoUrl, setLogoUrl] = useState("");
  const [corPrimaria, setCorPrimaria] = useState("");
  const [corSecundaria, setCorSecundaria] = useState("");
  const [corFundo, setCorFundo] = useState("");
  const [corTexto, setCorTexto] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    setNomeFantasia(branding?.identidadeVisual ?? "");
    setLogoUrl(branding?.urlLogo ?? "");
    setCorPrimaria(branding?.corPrimaria ?? "");
    setCorSecundaria(branding?.corSecundaria ?? "");
    setCorFundo(branding?.corTerciaria ?? "");
  }, [branding]);

  async function handleSubmit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    await onSubmit({
      nomeFantasia: nomeFantasia.trim() || null,
      logoUrl: logoUrl.trim() || null,
      corPrimaria: corPrimaria.trim() || null,
      corSecundaria: corSecundaria.trim() || null,
      corFundo: corFundo.trim() || null,
      corTexto: corTexto.trim() || null,
    });
  }

  return (
    <form className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border bg-white p-6" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      <div>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold">Branding</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600">Atualize as informações visuais usadas pelo locatário.</p>
      </div>
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Nome fantasia" value={nomeFantasia} onChange={(event) => setNomeFantasia(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="URL do logo" value={logoUrl} onChange={(event) => setLogoUrl(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor primária. Ex.: #2563eb" value={corPrimaria} onChange={(event) => setCorPrimaria(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor secundária. Ex.: #1d4ed8" value={corSecundaria} onChange={(event) => setCorSecundaria(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor de fundo. Ex.: #ffffff" value={corFundo} onChange={(event) => setCorFundo(event.target.value)} />
      <input className="rounded-xl border px-3 py-2" placeholder="Cor do texto. Ex.: #111827" value={corTexto} onChange={(event) => setCorTexto(event.target.value)} />
      <div className="flex justify-end">
        <button className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-2" type="submit" disabled={isSaving}>{isSaving ? "Salvando..." : "Salvar branding"}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}


===== apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingResumo.tsx =====

// apps/web/src/features/superadmin/locatarios/components/LocatarioBrandingResumo.tsx

import type { BrandingLocatario } from "../api/locatarioConfiguracoesApi";

type Props = { branding: BrandingLocatario | null };

export function LocatarioBrandingResumo({ branding }: Props) {
